#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p = Path('cholesky/cholesky.cpp')
s = p.read_text().replace('#include <algorithm>\\n', '#include <algorithm>\\n#include <atomic>\\n').replace('#include <vector>\\n', '#include <vector>\\n\\n#include <omp.h>\\n')
start = s.index('// Simple Cholesky decomposition')
end = s.index('void printUsage(')
s = s[:start] + r'''namespace {

constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;

// Avoid creating more workers than these matrix sizes can keep busy. The
// OpenMP runtime (including OMP_NUM_THREADS) still controls the upper limit.
int workerCount(const size_t n) {
    const size_t useful = std::max(size_t{1}, (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize);
    return static_cast<int>(std::min(useful, static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads())));
}

// C = X Y"'^T, or C -= X Y''^T. All matrices have row-major leading dimension n.
// Eight independent dot products share their input loads in the main kernel.
// Tiling keeps the panel data in cache; SIMD operates on the contiguous depth.
template <bool subtract>
void multiplyTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y,
                  const size_t n, const size_t rows, const size_t cols,
                  const size_t depth) {
    size_t i = 0;
    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
        size_t j = 0;
        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
            }
            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
            double* c1 = c0 + n;
            if constexpr (subtract) {
                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
            } else {
                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
            }
        }
        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
            const double* y = Y + j * n;
            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
            }
            if constexpr (subtract) {
                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
            } else {
                C[i * n + j] = s0;
                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
            }
        }
    }
    if (i < rows) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
            }
            if constexpr (subtract) {
                C[i * n + j] -= sum;
            } else {
                C[i * n + j] = sum;
            }
        }
    }
}

// Factor a diagonal tile after all earlier panel updates have completed.
// Return its size on success, or the local index of a nonpositive pivot.
size_t factorDiagonal(double* tile, const size_t n, const size_t size) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                sum += tile[i * n + k] * tile[j * n + k];
            }
            const double value = tile[i * n + j] - sum;
            if (i == j) {
                if (value <= 0.0) {
                    return i;
                }
                tile[i * n + j] = std::sqrt(value);
            } else {
                tile[i * n + j] = value / tile[j * n + j];
            }
        }
    }
    return size;
}

// Solve L_ik L_kk''^T = A_ik; rows of this panel tile are independent.
void solvePanel(double* panel, const double* diagonal, const size_t n,
                const size_t rows, const size_t width) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
        double* row = panel + i * n;
        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                sum += row[k] * diagonal[j * n + k];
            }
            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / diagonal[j * n + j];
        }
    }
}

} // namespace

// In-place, tiled Cholesky decomposition: A = L L''^T. Tasks belonging to
// different tiles can run concurrently. Each tile'"'s first element is its
// dependency token, so updates to a tile occur in increasing panel order.
bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    double* data = A.data();
    std::atomic<size_t> failedPivot{n};
    const int workers = workerCount(n);

    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(workers)
    {
        #pragma omp single
        {
            #pragma omp taskgroup
            {
                for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += tileSize) {
                    const size_t width = std::min(tileSize, n - k);
                    const size_t diagonal = k * n + k;
                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(k,width,diagonal) depend(inout:data[diagonal]) shared(failedPivot)
                    {
                        if (failedPivot.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n) {
                            const size_t bad = factorDiagonal(data + diagonal, n, width);
                            if (bad "'!= width) {
                                failedPivot.store(k + bad, std::memory_order_relaxed);
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    for (size_t i = k + width; i < n; i += tileSize) {
                        const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
                        const size_t panel = i * n + k;
                        #pragma omp task firstprivate(rows,width,diagonal,panel) depend(in:data[diagonal]) depend(inout:data[panel]) shared(failedPivot)
                        {
                            if (failedPivot.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n) {
                                solvePanel(data + panel, data + diagonal, n, rows, width);
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    for (size_t j = k + width; j < n; j += tileSize) {
                        const size_t cols = std::min(tileSize, n - j);
                        const size_t right = j * n + k;
                        for (size_t i = j; i < n; i += tileSize) {
                            const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
                            const size_t left = i * n + k;
                            const size_t output = i * n + j;
                            #pragma omp task firstprivate(rows,cols,width,left,right,output) depend(in:data[left],data[right]) depend(inout:data[output]) shared(failedPivot)
                            {
                                if (failedPivot.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n) {
                                    multiplyTile<true>(data + output, data + left,
                                                       data + right, n, rows, cols, width);
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // All tasks are finished before clearing the upper triangle.
        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
        }
    }

    const size_t bad = failedPivot.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    if (bad != n) {
        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu'"\\n\", bad);
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// Generate the same seeded random B, then compute A = B B"'^T + n I.
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    // rand_r mutates its seed: preserve the original sequence and ordering.
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }

    const size_t tiles = (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize;
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1) num_threads(workerCount(n))
    for (size_t t = 0; t < tiles * tiles; ++t) {
        const size_t i = (t / tiles) * tileSize;
        const size_t j = (t % tiles) * tileSize;
        if (j > i) {
            continue;
        }
        const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
        const size_t cols = std::min(tileSize, n - j);
        multiplyTile<false>(A.data() + i * n + j, B.data() + i * n,
                            B.data() + j * n, n, rows, cols, n);
        // Each lower tile owns its reflected upper tile as well.
        for (size_t r = 0; r < rows; ++r) {
            for (size_t c = 0; c < cols; ++c) {
                if (i + r > j + c) {
                    A[(j + c) * n + i + r] = A[(i + r) * n + j + c];
                }
            }
        }
        if (i == j) {
            for (size_t r = 0; r < rows; ++r) {
                A[(i + r) * n + i + r] += n;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    const size_t tiles = (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize;

    // Reconstruct tiles directly into thread-local storage, and compare both
    // symmetric entries. No full temporary matrix or extra cubic work is needed.
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1) num_threads(workerCount(n)) reduction(max:maxError,relError)
    for (size_t t = 0; t < tiles * tiles; ++t) {
        const size_t i = (t / tiles) * tileSize;
        const size_t j = (t % tiles) * tileSize;
        if (j > i) {
            continue;
        }
        const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
        const size_t cols = std::min(tileSize, n - j);
        // The shared kernel uses the same leading dimension for all operands.
        // A compact buffer can instead be filled one pair of rows at a time.
        for (size_t r = 0; r < rows; ++r) {
            for (size_t c = 0; c < cols; ++c) {
                if (i + r < j + c) {
                    continue;
                }
                double sum = 0.0;
                const size_t depth = j + c + 1;
                #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
                for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                    sum += L[(i + r) * n + k] * L[(j + c) * n + k];
                }
                const double original = A_orig[(i + r) * n + j + c];
                const double error = std::fabs(sum - original);
                maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
                relError = std::max(relError, error / (std::fabs(original) + 1e-10));
                if (i + r != j + c) {
                    const double reflected = A_orig[(j + c) * n + i + r];
                    const double reflectedError = std::fabs(sum - reflected);
                    maxError = std::max(maxError, reflectedError);
                    relError = std::max(relError, reflectedError / (std::fabs(reflected) + 1e-10));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e'"\\n\", maxError);
    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

codex
The first version passes validation through 2048×2048 and produces identical results across thread counts. The 1024×1024 factorization dropped from 254 ms to 7 ms with 16 threads. I’m tuning the tile-update kernel next to improve throughput.
exec
/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p = Path('cholesky/cholesky.cpp')
s = p.read_text()
where = s.index('// Factor a diagonal tile')
s = s[:where] + r'''// Pack the right panel so SIMD can update adjacent output columns without
// horizontal reductions. Four rows by eight columns stay in registers across
// the depth loop, reusing each packed load and each left-panel coefficient.
void updateTrailingTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y,
                        const size_t n, const size_t rows, const size_t cols,
                        const size_t depth) {
    alignas(64) double packed[tileSize * tileSize];
    for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
            packed[k * tileSize + j] = Y[j * n + k];
        }
    }

    size_t i = 0;
    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
        size_t j = 0;
        for (; j + 7 < cols; j += 8) {
            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                const double* right = packed + k * tileSize + j;
                const double x0 = X[i * n + k];
                const double x1 = X[(i + 1) * n + k];
                const double x2 = X[(i + 2) * n + k];
                const double x3 = X[(i + 3) * n + k];
                #pragma omp simd
                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
                    s0[c] += x0 * right[c];
                    s1[c] += x1 * right[c];
                    s2[c] += x2 * right[c];
                    s3[c] += x3 * right[c];
                }
            }
            #pragma omp simd
            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
                C[i * n + j + c] -= s0[c];
                C[(i + 1) * n + j + c] -= s1[c];
                C[(i + 2) * n + j + c] -= s2[c];
                C[(i + 3) * n + j + c] -= s3[c];
            }
        }
        if (j < cols) {
            multiplyTile<true>(C + i * n + j, X + i * n,
                               Y + j * n, n, 4, cols - j, depth);
        }
    }
    if (i < rows) {
        multiplyTile<true>(C + i * n, X + i * n, Y,
                           n, rows - i, cols, depth);
    }
}

''' + s[where:]
s = s.replace('multiplyTile<true>(data + output, data + left,\\n                                                       data + right, n, rows, cols, width);', 'updateTrailingTile(data + output, data + left,\\n                                                       data + right, n, rows, cols, width);')
s = s.replace('// Reconstruct tiles directly into thread-local storage, and compare both\\n    // symmetric entries. No full temporary matrix or extra cubic work is needed.', '// Reconstruct only the lower triangle and compare both symmetric entries.\\n    // Fuse comparison with reconstruction instead of allocating another matrix.')
s = s.replace('        // The shared kernel uses the same leading dimension for all operands.\\n        // A compact buffer can instead be filled one pair of rows at a time.\\n', '')
p.write_text(s)
PY
cmake --build /tmp/cholesky-build -j 4 && python3 - <<'PY'
import os, subprocess, time
for n in (512, 1024, 2048, 4096):
    for threads in (1, 4, 8, 16, 32):
        env = dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS=str(threads), OMP_PROC_BIND='close', OMP_PLACES='cores')
        start = time.perf_counter()

/bin/bash -lc "g++ -O3 -march=native -std=c++20 -fopenmp -Dmain=parallelMain -c cholesky/cholesky.cpp -o /tmp/cholesky-parallel.o && g++ -O3 -march=native -std=c++20 -fopenmp -x c++ - -x none /tmp/cholesky-parallel.o /tmp/cholesky-reference.o -o /tmp/cholesky-check <<'CPP'
#include <algorithm>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <omp.h>
bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>&, size_t);
bool referenceCholesky(std::vector<double>&, size_t);
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>&, size_t);
void referenceGenerate(std::vector<double>&, size_t);
int main() {
    for (size_t n : {0, 1, 2, 7, 31, 63, 64, 65, 127, 128, 129, 255, 256, 257, 511, 512, 513, 1025}) {
        std::vector<double> original(n*n), generated(n*n);
        referenceGenerate(original, n);
        generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(generated, n);
        double inputError = 0;
        for (size_t p = 0; p < n*n; ++p) {
            inputError = std::max(inputError, std::fabs(generated[p] - original[p]) / std::max(1.0, std::fabs(original[p])));
            assert(std::fabs(generated[p] - original[p]) < 1e-11 * std::max(1.0, std::fabs(original[p])));
        }
        auto reference = original;
        assert(referenceCholesky(reference, n));
        std::vector<double> first;
        double factorError = 0;
        for (int threads : {1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32}) {
            omp_set_num_threads(threads);
            auto result = original;
            assert(choleskyDecomposition(result, n));
            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
                for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
                    const size_t p = i*n+j;
                    factorError = std::max(factorError, std::fabs(result[p] - reference[p]));
                    assert(std::fabs(result[p] - reference[p]) < 1e-11 * std::max(1.0, std::fabs(reference[p])));
                    if (j > i) assert(result[p] == 0.0);
                }
            }
            if (first.empty()) first = result;
            else assert(result == first);
        }
        std::printf(\"n=%zu input relative difference %.3e, factor absolute difference %.3e\\n\", n, inputError, factorError);
    }
    for (size_t n : {1, 2, 63, 64, 65, 129, 257}) {
        for (size_t pivot : {size_t{0}, n/2, n-1}) {
            for (int threads : {1, 4, 16}) {
                omp_set_num_threads(threads);
                std::vector<double> a(n*n, 0.0);
                for (size_t i=0; i<n; ++i) a[i*n+i] = i == pivot ? -1.0 : 1.0;
                auto b = a;
                assert("'!referenceCholesky(a,n));
                assert(!choleskyDecomposition(b,n));
            }
        }
    }
    std::puts("Full-matrix reference comparisons, deterministic thread results, and failing pivots: PASSED");
}

/bin/bash -lc "g++ -O3 -march=native -std=c++20 -fopenmp -x c++ - -x none /tmp/cholesky-parallel.o -o /tmp/cholesky-timing <<'CPP'
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <vector>
#include <omp.h>
bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>&, size_t);
int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = argc > 1 ? std::strtoul(argv[1], nullptr, 10) : 2048;
    std::vector<double> a(n*n);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) a[i*n+j] = i == j ? n+1.0 : 0.01*std::sin(double(i+j));
    }
    for (int t : {1,2,4,8,16,32,64}) {
        omp_set_num_threads(t);
        double best = 1e9;
        for (int r = 0; r < 3; ++r) {
            auto b = a;
            auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
            if ("'!choleskyDecomposition(b,n)) return 1;
            double sec = std::chrono::duration<double>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start).count();
            best = std::min(best, sec);
        }
        std::printf("n=%zu threads=%d time=%.3f ms %.2f GFLOPS'"\\n\",n,t,best*1e3,double(n)*n*n/3/best/1e9);
        std::fflush(stdout);
    }
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <atomic>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include <omp.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

namespace {

constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;

// Avoid creating more workers than these matrix sizes can keep busy. The
// OpenMP runtime (including OMP_NUM_THREADS) still controls the upper limit.
int workerCount(const size_t n) {
    const size_t useful = std::max(size_t{1}, (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize);
    return static_cast<int>(std::min(useful, static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads())));
}

// C = X Y^T, or C -= X Y^T. All matrices have row-major leading dimension n.
// Eight independent dot products share their input loads in the main kernel.
// Tiling keeps the panel data in cache; SIMD operates on the contiguous depth.
template <bool subtract>
void multiplyTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y,
                  const size_t n, const size_t rows, const size_t cols,
                  const size_t depth) {
    size_t i = 0;
    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
        size_t j = 0;
        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
            }
            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
            double* c1 = c0 + n;
            if constexpr (subtract) {
                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
            } else {
                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
            }
        }
        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
            const double* y = Y + j * n;
            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
            }
            if constexpr (subtract) {
                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
            } else {
                C[i * n + j] = s0;
                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
            }
        }
    }
    if (i < rows) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
            }
            if constexpr (subtract) {
                C[i * n + j] -= sum;
            } else {
                C[i * n + j] = sum;
            }
        }
    }
}

// The right panel is transposed into the otherwise unused upper triangle.
// Four rows by eight columns stay in registers across the depth loop,
// reusing panel coefficients without horizontal SIMD reductions.
void updateTrailingTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y,
                        const size_t n, const size_t rows, const size_t cols,
                        const size_t depth) {
    size_t i = 0;
    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
        size_t j = 0;
        for (; j + 7 < cols; j += 8) {
            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
            #if defined(__GNUC__)
            #pragma GCC unroll 1
            #endif
            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                const double* right = Y + k * n + j;
                const double x0 = X[i * n + k];
                const double x1 = X[(i + 1) * n + k];
                const double x2 = X[(i + 2) * n + k];
                const double x3 = X[(i + 3) * n + k];
                #pragma omp simd
                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
                    s0[c] += x0 * right[c];
                    s1[c] += x1 * right[c];
                    s2[c] += x2 * right[c];
                    s3[c] += x3 * right[c];
                }
            }
            #pragma omp simd
            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
                C[i * n + j + c] -= s0[c];
                C[(i + 1) * n + j + c] -= s1[c];
                C[(i + 2) * n + j + c] -= s2[c];
                C[(i + 3) * n + j + c] -= s3[c];
            }
        }
        if (j < cols) {
            for (size_t r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
                for (size_t c = j; c < cols; ++c) {
                    double sum = 0.0;
                    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                        sum += X[(i + r) * n + k] * Y[k * n + c];
                    }
                    C[(i + r) * n + c] -= sum;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[k * n + j];
            }
            C[i * n + j] -= sum;
        }
    }
}

// Factor a diagonal tile after all earlier panel updates have completed.
// Return its size on success, or the local index of a nonpositive pivot.
size_t factorDiagonal(double* tile, const size_t n, const size_t size) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                sum += tile[i * n + k] * tile[j * n + k];
            }
            const double value = tile[i * n + j] - sum;
            if (i == j) {
                if (value <= 0.0) {
                    return i;
                }
                tile[i * n + j] = std::sqrt(value);
            } else {
                tile[i * n + j] = value / tile[j * n + j];
            }
        }
    }
    return size;
}

// Solve L_ik L_kk^T = A_ik; rows of this panel tile are independent.
void solvePanel(double* panel, const double* diagonal, double* transposed, const size_t n,
                const size_t rows, const size_t width) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
        double* row = panel + i * n;
        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                sum += row[k] * diagonal[j * n + k];
            }
            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / diagonal[j * n + j];
            transposed[j * n + i] = row[j];
        }
    }
}

} // namespace

// In-place, tiled Cholesky decomposition: A = L L^T. Tasks belonging to
// different tiles can run concurrently. Each tile's first element is its
// dependency token, so updates to a tile occur in increasing panel order.
bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    double* data = A.data();
    std::atomic<size_t> failedPivot{n};
    const int workers = workerCount(n);

    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(workers)
    {
        #pragma omp single
        {
            #pragma omp taskgroup
            {
                for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += tileSize) {
                    const size_t width = std::min(tileSize, n - k);
                    const size_t diagonal = k * n + k;
                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(k,width,diagonal) depend(inout:data[diagonal]) shared(failedPivot)
                    {
                        if (failedPivot.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n) {
                            const size_t bad = factorDiagonal(data + diagonal, n, width);
                            if (bad != width) {
                                failedPivot.store(k + bad, std::memory_order_relaxed);
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    for (size_t i = k + width; i < n; i += tileSize) {
                        const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
                        const size_t panel = i * n + k;
                        const size_t transposed = k * n + i;
                        #pragma omp task firstprivate(rows,width,diagonal,panel,transposed) depend(in:data[diagonal]) depend(inout:data[panel]) shared(failedPivot)
                        {
                            if (failedPivot.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n) {
                                solvePanel(data + panel, data + diagonal, data + transposed, n, rows, width);
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    for (size_t j = k + width; j < n; j += tileSize) {
                        const size_t cols = std::min(tileSize, n - j);
                        const size_t right = j * n + k;
                        const size_t rightTransposed = k * n + j;
                        for (size_t i = j; i < n; i += tileSize) {
                            const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
                            const size_t left = i * n + k;
                            const size_t output = i * n + j;
                            #pragma omp task firstprivate(rows,cols,width,left,right,rightTransposed,output) depend(in:data[left],data[right]) depend(inout:data[output]) shared(failedPivot)
                            {
                                if (failedPivot.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n) {
                                    updateTrailingTile(data + output, data + left,
                                                       data + rightTransposed, n, rows, cols, width);
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // All tasks are finished before clearing the upper triangle.
        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
        }
    }

    const size_t bad = failedPivot.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    if (bad != n) {
        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", bad);
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// Generate the same seeded random B, then compute A = B B^T + n I.
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    // rand_r mutates its seed: preserve the original sequence and ordering.
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }

    const size_t tiles = (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize;
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1) num_threads(workerCount(n))
    for (size_t t = 0; t < tiles * tiles; ++t) {
        const size_t i = (t / tiles) * tileSize;
        const size_t j = (t % tiles) * tileSize;
        if (j > i) {
            continue;
        }
        const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
        const size_t cols = std::min(tileSize, n - j);
        multiplyTile<false>(A.data() + i * n + j, B.data() + i * n,
                            B.data() + j * n, n, rows, cols, n);
        // Each lower tile owns its reflected upper tile as well.
        for (size_t r = 0; r < rows; ++r) {
            for (size_t c = 0; c < cols; ++c) {
                if (i + r > j + c) {
                    A[(j + c) * n + i + r] = A[(i + r) * n + j + c];
                }
            }
        }
        if (i == j) {
            for (size_t r = 0; r < rows; ++r) {
                A[(i + r) * n + i + r] += n;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    const size_t tiles = (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize;

    // Reconstruct only the lower triangle and compare both symmetric entries.
    // Fuse comparison with reconstruction instead of allocating another matrix.
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1) num_threads(workerCount(n)) reduction(max:maxError,relError)
    for (size_t t = 0; t < tiles * tiles; ++t) {
        const size_t i = (t / tiles) * tileSize;
        const size_t j = (t % tiles) * tileSize;
        if (j > i) {
            continue;
        }
        const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
        const size_t cols = std::min(tileSize, n - j);
        for (size_t r = 0; r < rows; ++r) {
            for (size_t c = 0; c < cols; ++c) {
                if (i + r < j + c) {
                    continue;
                }
                double sum = 0.0;
                const size_t depth = j + c + 1;
                #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
                for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                    sum += L[(i + r) * n + k] * L[(j + c) * n + k];
                }
                const double original = A_orig[(i + r) * n + j + c];
                const double error = std::fabs(sum - original);
                maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
                relError = std::max(relError, error / (std::fabs(original) + 1e-10));
                if (i + r != j + c) {
                    const double reflected = A_orig[(j + c) * n + i + r];
                    const double reflectedError = std::fabs(sum - reflected);
                    maxError = std::max(maxError, reflectedError);
                    relError = std::max(relError, reflectedError / (std::fabs(reflected) + 1e-10));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

from pathlib import Path
import subprocess, os
source = Path('cholesky/cholesky.cpp').read_text().replace('int main(int argc, char** argv)', 'int benchmarkMain(int argc, char** argv)')
harness = r'''
int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    const size_t n=std::strtoul(argv[1],nullptr,10);
    std::vector<double> a(n*n);
    for (size_t i=0;i<n;++i) for (size_t j=0;j<n;++j)
        a[i*n+j]= i==j ? n+1.0 : 0.01*std::sin(double(i+j));
    for (int threads : {1,4,8,16,32,64}) {
        if (n==4096 && threads<16) continue;
        omp_set_num_threads(threads);
        double best=1e9;
        for (int r=0;r<3;++r) {
            auto b=a;
            const auto start=std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
            if ("'!choleskyDecomposition(b,n)) return 1;
            const double sec=std::chrono::duration<double>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now()-start).count();
            best=std::min(best,sec);
        }
        printf("n=%zu threads=%d %.3f ms %.2f GFLOPS'"\\n\",n,threads,best*1e3,double(n)*n*n/3/best/1e9);
        fflush(stdout);
    }
}
'''
variants = {
    'packed64':source,
    'packed64_unroll':source.replace('            #if defined(__GNUC__)\\n            #pragma GCC unroll 1\\n            #endif\\n',''),
    'packed128':source.replace('constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;', 'constexpr size_t tileSize = 128;'),
}
env=dict(os.environ,OMP_PROC_BIND='close',OMP_PLACES='cores')
for name,s in variants.items():
    exe='/tmp/cholesky-'+name
    subprocess.run(['g++','-O3','-march=native','-std=c++20','-fopenmp','-Icholesky','-x','c++','-','-o',exe],input=s+harness,text=True,check=True)
    print(name,flush=True)
    for n in (1024,2048,4096):
        subprocess.run([exe,str(n)],env=env,check=True)

from pathlib import Path
p=Path('cholesky/cholesky.cpp')
s=p.read_text()
s=s.replace('constexpr size_t packedStride = tileSize + 8;\\nconstexpr size_t packedTileElements = tileSize * packedStride;\\n','')
s=s.replace('int workerCount(const size_t n) {\\n    const size_t useful = std::max(size_t{1}, (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize);', 'int workerCount(const size_t n, const size_t blockSize = tileSize) {\\n    const size_t useful = std::max(size_t{1}, (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize);')

    size_t i = 0;
    for (; i + 3 < rows;''', '''                        const size_t depth, const size_t packedStride) {
    size_t i = 0;
    for (; i + 3 < rows;''')
s=s.replace('            #if defined(__GNUC__)\\n            #pragma GCC unroll 1\\n            #endif\\n', '')
s=s.replace('size_t factorDiagonal(double* tile, const size_t n, const size_t size) {\\n', 'size_t factorDiagonal(double* tile, const size_t n, const size_t size) {\\n    double inverse[2 * tileSize];\\n')
s=s.replace('                tile[i * n + j] = std::sqrt(value);', '                tile[i * n + j] = std::sqrt(value);\\n                inverse[i] = 1.0 / tile[i * n + j];')
s=s.replace('tile[i * n + j] = value / tile[j * n + j];', 'tile[i * n + j] = value * inverse[j];')
start=s.index('void solvePanel(')
end=s.index('\\n} // namespace',start)
s=s[:start]+r'''void solvePanel(double* panel, const double* diagonal, double* transposed,
                const size_t n, const size_t rows, const size_t width,
                const size_t packedStride) {
    double inverse[2 * tileSize];
    for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
        inverse[j] = 1.0 / diagonal[j * n + j];
    }
    size_t i = 0;
    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
        double* row0 = panel + i * n;
        double* row1 = row0 + n;
        double* row2 = row1 + n;
        double* row3 = row2 + n;
        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
            const double* d = diagonal + j * n;
            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0, s2 = 0.0, s3 = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1,s2,s3)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                s0 += row0[k] * d[k];
                s1 += row1[k] * d[k];
                s2 += row2[k] * d[k];
                s3 += row3[k] * d[k];
            }
            row0[j] = (row0[j] - s0) * inverse[j];
            row1[j] = (row1[j] - s1) * inverse[j];
            row2[j] = (row2[j] - s2) * inverse[j];
            row3[j] = (row3[j] - s3) * inverse[j];
            double* packedRow = transposed + j * packedStride + i;
            packedRow[0] = row0[j];
            packedRow[1] = row1[j];
            packedRow[2] = row2[j];
            packedRow[3] = row3[j];
        }
    }
    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
        double* row = panel + i * n;
        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                sum += row[k] * diagonal[j * n + k];
            }
            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) * inverse[j];
            transposed[j * packedStride + i] = row[j];
        }
    }
}
''' + s[end:]
start=s.index('bool choleskyDecomposition(')
end=s.index('// Generate the same seeded',start)
part=s[start:end].replace('    const size_t tiles = (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize;', '''    // Larger panels amortize task overhead once there is ample parallel work.
    const size_t blockSize = n >= 4096 ? 2 * tileSize : tileSize;
    const size_t packedStride = blockSize + 8;
    const size_t packedTileElements = blockSize * packedStride;
    const size_t tiles = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize;''')
part=part.replace('const int workers = workerCount(n);', 'const int workers = workerCount(n, blockSize);')
part=part.replace('k += tileSize', 'k += blockSize').replace('i += tileSize', 'i += blockSize').replace('j += tileSize', 'j += blockSize')
part=part.replace('std::min(tileSize,', 'std::min(blockSize,').replace('i / tileSize','i / blockSize').replace('j / tileSize','j / blockSize').replace('k / tileSize','k / blockSize')
part=part.replace('packed + transposed, n, rows, width);', 'packed + transposed, n, rows, width, packedStride);')
part=part.replace('packed + rightTransposed, n, rows, cols, width);', 'packed + rightTransposed, n, rows, cols, width, packedStride);')
s=s[:start]+part+s[end:]
p.write_text(s)

#define main parallelBenchmarkMain
#include \"cholesky/cholesky.cpp\"
#undef main
#include <cassert>
bool referenceCholesky(std::vector<double>&, size_t);
void referenceGenerate(std::vector<double>&, size_t);
int main() {
    for (size_t n : {0,1,2,7,31,63,64,65,127,128,129,255,256,257,511,512,513,1025}) {
        std::vector<double> original(n*n), generated(n*n);
        referenceGenerate(original,n);
        generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(generated,n);
        for (size_t p=0;p<n*n;++p)
            assert(std::fabs(generated[p]-original[p]) < 1e-11*std::max(1.0,std::fabs(original[p])));
        auto reference=original;
        assert(referenceCholesky(reference,n));
        std::vector<double> first;
        double error=0;
        for (int threads : {1,2,4,8,16,32}) {
            omp_set_num_threads(threads);
            auto a=original;
            assert(choleskyDecomposition(a,n));
            for (size_t i=0;i<n;++i) for (size_t j=0;j<n;++j) {
                size_t p=i*n+j;
                error=std::max(error,std::fabs(a[p]-reference[p]));
                assert(std::fabs(a[p]-reference[p]) < 1e-11*std::max(1.0,std::fabs(reference[p])));
                if (j>i) assert(a[p]==0.0);
            }
            if (first.empty()) first=a;
            else assert(a==first);
        }
        printf(\"Reference comparison n=%zu: PASSED (max difference %.3e)\\n\",n,error);
    }
    for (size_t n : {1,65,129,257}) {
        for (size_t pivot : {size_t{0},n/2,n-1}) {
            for (int threads : {1,16}) {
                omp_set_num_threads(threads);
                std::vector<double> a(n*n,0.0);
                for (size_t i=0;i<n;++i) a[i*n+i]=i==pivot ? 0.0 : 1.0;
                auto b=a;
                assert("'!referenceCholesky(a,n));
                assert(!choleskyDecomposition(b,n));
            }
        }
    }
    // Exercise the larger panel layout and its final partial tile.
    const size_t n=4097;
    std::vector<double> a(n*n,0.0);
    for (size_t i=0;i<n;++i) {
        a[i*n+i]=4.0;
        if (i) a[i*n+i-1]=a[(i-1)*n+i]=1.0;
    }
    omp_set_num_threads(32);
    const auto original=a;
    assert(choleskyDecomposition(a,n));
    assert(validateCholesky(a,original,n));
    // Validation must still catch a wrong factor or an asymmetric original.
    a[0]*=1.1;
    assert(!validateCholesky(a,original,n));
    puts("Reference equality within tolerance, thread determinism, invalid pivots, and large partial tiles: PASSED");
