    17	constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
    18	
    19	// Avoid creating more workers than these matrix sizes can keep busy. The
    20	// OpenMP runtime (including OMP_NUM_THREADS) still controls the upper limit.
    21	int workerCount(const size_t n, const size_t blockSize = tileSize) {
    22	    const size_t useful = std::max(size_t{1}, (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize);
    23	    return static_cast<int>(std::min(useful, static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads())));
    24	}
    25	
    26	// C = X Y^T. All matrices have row-major leading dimension n.
    27	// Eight independent dot products share their input loads in the main kernel.
    28	// Tiling keeps the panel data in cache; SIMD operates on the contiguous depth.
    29	void multiplyTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y,
    30	                  const size_t n, const size_t rows, const size_t cols,
    31	                  const size_t depth) {
    32	    size_t i = 0;
    33	    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
    34	        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
    35	        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
    36	        size_t j = 0;
    37	        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
    38	            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
    39	            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
    40	            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
    41	            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
    42	            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
    43	            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
    44	            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
    45	            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
    46	                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
    47	                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
    48	                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
    49	                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
    50	                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
    51	                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
    52	                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
    53	                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
    54	            }
    55	            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
    56	            double* c1 = c0 + n;
    57	            c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
    58	            c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
    59	        }
    60	        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
    61	            const double* y = Y + j * n;
    62	            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
    63	            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
    64	            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
    65	                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
    66	                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
    67	            }
    68	            C[i * n + j] = s0;
    69	            C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
    70	        }
    71	    }
    72	    if (i < rows) {
    73	        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
    74	            double sum = 0.0;
    75	            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
    76	            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
    77	                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
    78	            }
    79	            C[i * n + j] = sum;
    80	        }
    81	    }
    82	}
    83	
    84	// Panel tiles are packed once in transposed order with a padded stride to
    85	// avoid cache-set conflicts. Four rows by eight columns accumulate across
    86	// the depth loop, reusing coefficients without horizontal SIMD reductions.
    87	void updateTrailingTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y,
    88	                        const size_t n, const size_t rows, const size_t cols,
    89	                        const size_t depth, const size_t packedStride) {
    90	    size_t i = 0;
    91	    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
    92	        size_t j = 0;
    93	        for (; j + 7 < cols; j += 8) {
    94	            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
    95	            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
    96	                const double* right = Y + k * packedStride + j;
    97	                const double x0 = X[i * n + k];
    98	                const double x1 = X[(i + 1) * n + k];
    99	                const double x2 = X[(i + 2) * n + k];
   100	                const double x3 = X[(i + 3) * n + k];
   101	                #pragma omp simd
   102	                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
   103	                    s0[c] += x0 * right[c];
   104	                    s1[c] += x1 * right[c];
   105	                    s2[c] += x2 * right[c];
   106	                    s3[c] += x3 * right[c];
   107	                }
   108	            }
   109	            #pragma omp simd
   110	            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
   111	                C[i * n + j + c] -= s0[c];
   112	                C[(i + 1) * n + j + c] -= s1[c];
   113	                C[(i + 2) * n + j + c] -= s2[c];
   114	                C[(i + 3) * n + j + c] -= s3[c];
   115	            }
   116	        }
   117	        if (j < cols) {
   118	            for (size_t r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
   119	                for (size_t c = j; c < cols; ++c) {
   120	                    double sum = 0.0;
   121	                    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
   122	                        sum += X[(i + r) * n + k] * Y[k * packedStride + c];
   123	                    }
   124	                    C[(i + r) * n + c] -= sum;
   125	                }
   126	            }
   127	        }
   128	    }
   129	    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
   130	        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
   131	            double sum = 0.0;
   132	            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
   133	                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[k * packedStride + j];
   134	            }
   135	            C[i * n + j] -= sum;
   136	        }
   137	    }
   138	}
   139	
   140	// Factor a diagonal tile after all earlier panel updates have completed.
   141	// Return its size on success, or the local index of a nonpositive pivot.
   142	size_t factorDiagonal(double* tile, const size_t n, const size_t size) {
   143	    double inverse[2 * tileSize];
   144	    for (size_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
   145	        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
   146	            double sum = 0.0;
   147	            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
   148	            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
   149	                sum += tile[i * n + k] * tile[j * n + k];
   150	            }
   151	            const double value = tile[i * n + j] - sum;
   152	            if (i == j) {
   153	                if (value <= 0.0) {
   154	                    return i;
   155	                }
   156	                tile[i * n + j] = std::sqrt(value);
   157	                inverse[i] = 1.0 / tile[i * n + j];
   158	            } else {
   159	                tile[i * n + j] = value * inverse[j];
   160	            }
   161	        }
   162	    }
   163	    return size;
   164	}
   165	
   166	// Solve L_ik L_kk^T = A_ik; rows of this panel tile are independent.
   167	void solvePanel(double* panel, const double* diagonal, double* transposed,
   168	                const size_t n, const size_t rows, const size_t width,
   169	                const size_t packedStride) {
   170	    double inverse[2 * tileSize];
   171	    for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
   172	        inverse[j] = 1.0 / diagonal[j * n + j];
   173	    }
   174	    size_t i = 0;
   175	    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
   176	        double* row0 = panel + i * n;
   177	        double* row1 = row0 + n;
   178	        double* row2 = row1 + n;
   179	        double* row3 = row2 + n;
   180	        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
   181	            const double* d = diagonal + j * n;
   182	            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0, s2 = 0.0, s3 = 0.0;
   183	            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1,s2,s3)
   184	            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
   185	                s0 += row0[k] * d[k];
   186	                s1 += row1[k] * d[k];
   187	                s2 += row2[k] * d[k];
   188	                s3 += row3[k] * d[k];
   189	            }
   190	            row0[j] = (row0[j] - s0) * inverse[j];
   191	            row1[j] = (row1[j] - s1) * inverse[j];
   192	            row2[j] = (row2[j] - s2) * inverse[j];
   193	            row3[j] = (row3[j] - s3) * inverse[j];
   194	            double* packedRow = transposed + j * packedStride + i;
   195	            packedRow[0] = row0[j];
   196	            packedRow[1] = row1[j];
   197	            packedRow[2] = row2[j];
   198	            packedRow[3] = row3[j];
   199	        }
   200	    }
   201	    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
   202	        double* row = panel + i * n;
   203	        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
   204	            double sum = 0.0;
   205	            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
   206	            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
   207	                sum += row[k] * diagonal[j * n + k];
   208	            }
   209	            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) * inverse[j];
   210	            transposed[j * packedStride + i] = row[j];
   211	        }
   212	    }
   213	}
   214	
   215	} // namespace
   216	
   217	// In-place, tiled Cholesky decomposition: A = L L^T. Tasks belonging to
   218	// different tiles can run concurrently. Each tile's first element is its
   219	// dependency token, so updates to a tile occur in increasing panel order.
   220	bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
   221	    double* data = A.data();
   222	    // Larger panels amortize task overhead once there is ample parallel work.
   223	    const size_t blockSize = n >= 4096 ? 2 * tileSize : tileSize;
   224	    const size_t packedStride = blockSize + 8;
   225	    const size_t packedTileElements = blockSize * packedStride;
   226	    const size_t tiles = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize;
   227	    // Only off-diagonal lower tiles need a packed copy. Allocate without
   228	    // initialization: each panel task fills its tile before any reader runs.
   229	    const size_t packedElements = tiles * (tiles == 0 ? 0 : tiles - 1) / 2 * packedTileElements;
   230	    std::unique_ptr<double[]> packedStorage(new double[packedElements]);
   231	    double* packed = packedStorage.get();
   232	    std::atomic<size_t> failedPivot{n};
   233	    const int workers = workerCount(n, blockSize);
   234	
   235	    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(workers)
   236	    {
   237	        #pragma omp single
   238	        {
   239	            #pragma omp taskgroup
   240	            {
   241	                for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += blockSize) {
   242	                    const size_t width = std::min(blockSize, n - k);
   243	                    const size_t diagonal = k * n + k;
   244	                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(k,width,diagonal) depend(inout:data[diagonal]) shared(failedPivot)
   245	                    {
   246	                        if (failedPivot.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n) {
   247	                            const size_t bad = factorDiagonal(data + diagonal, n, width);
   248	                            if (bad != width) {
   249	                                failedPivot.store(k + bad, std::memory_order_relaxed);
   250	                            }
   251	                        }
   252	                    }
   253	
   254	                    for (size_t i = k + width; i < n; i += blockSize) {
   255	                        const size_t rows = std::min(blockSize, n - i);
   256	                        const size_t panel = i * n + k;
   257	                        const size_t rowTile = i / blockSize;
   258	                        const size_t transposed = (rowTile * (rowTile - 1) / 2 + k / blockSize) * packedTileElements;
   259	                        #pragma omp task firstprivate(rows,width,diagonal,panel,transposed) depend(in:data[diagonal]) depend(inout:data[panel]) shared(failedPivot)
   260	                        {
   261	                            if (failedPivot.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n) {
   262	                                solvePanel(data + panel, data + diagonal, packed + transposed, n, rows, width, packedStride);
   263	                            }
   264	                        }
   265	                    }
   266	
   267	                    for (size_t j = k + width; j < n; j += blockSize) {
   268	                        const size_t cols = std::min(blockSize, n - j);
   269	                        const size_t right = j * n + k;
   270	                        const size_t colTile = j / blockSize;
   271	                        const size_t rightTransposed = (colTile * (colTile - 1) / 2 + k / blockSize) * packedTileElements;
   272	                        for (size_t i = j; i < n; i += blockSize) {
   273	                            const size_t rows = std::min(blockSize, n - i);
   274	                            const size_t left = i * n + k;
   275	                            const size_t output = i * n + j;
   276	                            #pragma omp task firstprivate(rows,cols,width,left,right,rightTransposed,output) depend(in:data[left],data[right]) depend(inout:data[output]) shared(failedPivot)
   277	                            {
   278	                                if (failedPivot.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n) {
   279	                                    updateTrailingTile(data + output, data + left,
   280	                                                       packed + rightTransposed, n, rows, cols, width, packedStride);
   281	                                }
   282	                            }
   283	                        }
   284	                    }
   285	                }
   286	            }
   287	        }
   288	
   289	        // All tasks are finished before clearing the upper triangle.
   290	        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
   291	        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   292	            std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
   293	        }
   294	    }
   295	
   296	    const size_t bad = failedPivot.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
   297	    if (bad != n) {
   298	        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", bad);
   299	        return false;
   300	    }
   301	    return true;
   302	}
   303	
   304	// Generate the same seeded random B, then compute A = B B^T + n I.
   305	void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
   306	    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
   307	    unsigned int seed = 42;
   308	    // rand_r mutates its seed: preserve the original sequence and ordering.
   309	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
   310	        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
   311	    }
   312	
   313	    const size_t tiles = (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize;
   314	    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1) num_threads(workerCount(n))
   315	    for (size_t t = 0; t < tiles * tiles; ++t) {
   316	        const size_t i = (t / tiles) * tileSize;
   317	        const size_t j = (t % tiles) * tileSize;
   318	        if (j > i) {
   319	            continue;
   320	        }
   321	        const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
   322	        const size_t cols = std::min(tileSize, n - j);
   323	        multiplyTile(A.data() + i * n + j, B.data() + i * n,
   324	                            B.data() + j * n, n, rows, cols, n);
   325	        // Each lower tile owns its reflected upper tile as well.
   326	        for (size_t r = 0; r < rows; ++r) {
   327	            for (size_t c = 0; c < cols; ++c) {
   328	                if (i + r > j + c) {
   329	                    A[(j + c) * n + i + r] = A[(i + r) * n + j + c];
   330	                }
   330	                }
   331	            }
   332	        }
   333	        if (i == j) {
   334	            for (size_t r = 0; r < rows; ++r) {
   335	                A[(i + r) * n + i + r] += n;
   336	            }
   337	        }
   338	    }
   339	}
   340	
   341	bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
   342	    double maxError = 0.0;
   343	    double relError = 0.0;
   344	    const size_t tiles = (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize;
   345	
   346	    // Reconstruct only the lower triangle and compare both symmetric entries.
   347	    // Fuse comparison with reconstruction instead of allocating another matrix.
   348	    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1) num_threads(workerCount(n)) reduction(max:maxError,relError)
   349	    for (size_t t = 0; t < tiles * tiles; ++t) {
   350	        const size_t i = (t / tiles) * tileSize;
   351	        const size_t j = (t % tiles) * tileSize;
   352	        if (j > i) {
   353	            continue;
   354	        }
   355	        const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
   356	        const size_t cols = std::min(tileSize, n - j);
   357	        for (size_t r = 0; r < rows; ++r) {
   358	            for (size_t c = 0; c < cols; ++c) {
   359	                if (i + r < j + c) {
   360	                    continue;
   361	                }
   362	                double sum = 0.0;
   363	                const size_t depth = j + c + 1;
   364	                #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
   365	                for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
   366	                    sum += L[(i + r) * n + k] * L[(j + c) * n + k];
   367	                }
   368	                const double original = A_orig[(i + r) * n + j + c];
   369	                const double error = std::fabs(sum - original);
   370	                maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
   371	                relError = std::max(relError, error / (std::fabs(original) + 1e-10));
   372	                if (i + r != j + c) {
   373	                    const double reflected = A_orig[(j + c) * n + i + r];
   374	                    const double reflectedError = std::fabs(sum - reflected);
   375	                    maxError = std::max(maxError, reflectedError);
   376	                    relError = std::max(relError, reflectedError / (std::fabs(reflected) + 1e-10));
   377	                }
   378	            }
   379	        }
   380	    }
   381	
   382	    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
   383	    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
   384	    if (relError > 1e-6) {
   385	        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
   386	        return false;
   387	    }
   388	    return true;
   389	}
   390	
   391	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   392	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   393	    printf("Options:\n");
   394	    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
   395	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   396	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   397	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   398	}
   399	
   400	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   401	    size_t n = 512;
   402	    bool validate = false;
   403	    bool printResults = false;
   404	    
   405	    // Parse command line arguments
   406	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   407	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   408	            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
   409	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   410	            validate = true;
   411	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   412	            printResults = true;
   413	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   414	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   415	            return 0;
   416	        } else {
   417	            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   418	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   419	            return 1;
   420	        }
   421	    }
   422	    
   423	    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
   424	    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
   425	    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   426	    
   427	    // Allocate matrix
   428	    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
   429	    std::vector<double> A_orig;
   430	    
   431	    // Generate positive definite matrix
   432	    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
   433	    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
   434	    
   435	    if (validate) {
   436	        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
   437	    }
   438	    
   439	    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
   440	    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
   441	    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   442	    
   443	    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
   444	    
   445	    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   446	    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
   447	    
   448	    if (!success) {
   449	        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
   450	        return 1;
   451	    }
   452	    
   453	    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
   454	    
   455	    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
   456	    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
   457	    const double seconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count();
   458	    double gflops = seconds > 0.0 ? ops / seconds / 1e9 : 0.0;
   459	    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   460	    
   461	    // Print results for external validation
   462	    if (printResults) {
   463	        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
   464	    }
   465	    
   466	    // Validation
   467	    if (validate) {
   468	        printf("Validating result...\n");
   469	        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
   470	        
   471	        if (valid) {
   472	            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   473	            return 0;
   474	        } else {
   475	            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   476	            return 1;
   477	        }
   478	    }
   479	    
   480	    return 0;
   481	}
